| Model | Mean held-out joint log score (nat/trial) | Paired mean difference from M0 | Participant SE of difference |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| M0 | -1.055131 | 0 | — |
| MV | -0.940120 | 0.115012 | 0.012956 |
| MZ | -1.015594 | 0.039537 | 0.007018 |
| MC | -0.952028 | 0.103104 | 0.011697 |
| MVZ | -0.933551 | 0.121581 | 0.013609 |
| MVC | -0.934507 | 0.120624 | 0.012985 |

Both held-out halves are summed after each half's log sum is divided by 256;
participants are then weighted equally. These are exploratory plug-in
within-session predictive scores. Participant SEs are descriptive, not a
recovery, superiority, cluster-interval or mechanism decision. The logistic
benchmark remains unimplemented; this report does not establish generally
best prediction. Descriptive access has exposed the observed outcomes, so
this report does not create a fresh confirmation set. M0 direction 0 retains
sub-107's lower kappa contact; MVC direction 0 retains the ER eta population
log-SD contact near its lower bound. These are disclosed, not repaired away.

### Oracle information result and the next fitted recovery component

The oracle diagnostic completed in 204.073 seconds in allocation `46424502`.
Every adaptive quadrature converged and probability/path-information checks
passed. [Quantitative evidence](runs/behavioral_mechanism_kill_test/20261003-oracle-information01/oracle_information.json):

| Five-point calibration | EI expected KL | ER expected KL | Participant mean KL | Mean full-path bound |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| DDM probability | 0.00401017 | 0.00459077 | 0.00430047 | 0.00446078 |
| Observed mixture probability | 0.00417853 | 0.00478116 | 0.00447985 | 0.00464690 |

All values are nats per trial, below the policy's 0.01 margin. These are
expected differences for the exact source offers and the recorded nuisance
draw, not empirical model scores. A best population null can be closer than
the same-nuisance comparator. Conversely, a finite-sample estimated null can
fit poorly and inflate apparent candidate gains. The calculation therefore
identifies a prospective feasibility concern; it establishes neither a
90-percent recovery probability nor a terminal failure of EP01.

The coordinator authorized the targeted fitted next component:
`code/run_criterion_recovery.py`. The generating model is fixed-bound MC at
1x SESOI; each participant's c changes **full mixture-level** acceptance by
exactly five percentage points at zero reference drift, with their drawn
boundary/nondecision parameters. This is the larger-effect calibration above.
All original offers, all 108 participants and both primary half directions
are retained. M0 and MC each learn their own training-only hierarchical
parameters under the same recorded fitting procedure. Primary comparison uses
participant-paired gain, the unchanged 0.01/two-SE rules, both task-version
signs and the participant median/95-percent interval. The interval uses 9,999
fixed-seed within-version participant draws, fixed before this fitted result.
No observed choice or RT enters these synthetic fits.

The 200-replicate plan predates any fitted recovery comparison. If 21 valid
replicates fail this necessary comparison, the best possible completed-cell
success count is 179/200, below 90 percent even if all remaining replicates
succeed. Recording this bound can curtail unnecessary computation without
changing the threshold; it does not establish the other recovery rules or a
whole-episode terminal state. More complex rivals cannot rescue a failure to
beat the nested null. Existing narrower and generic-bias branches remain
subject to their original prerequisites.

compute allocation `46424502`.

Before the representative MC fit starts, added reporting-only support
diagnostics to the hierarchical fitter: participant parameter contacts and
EI/ER log-standard-deviation contacts with declared bounds are retained in each
fit report, and the MC runner prints their counts. Contact means within
`1e-6 * (1 + bound width)` of an endpoint; this is a numerical diagnostic,
not an additional scientific exclusion. No objective, prior, optimization or
model-selection rule changed. The recorded implementation already requires
support contacts to be reported before gate interpretation.
